> 24	## 1. Conceptual Data Model

> 59	## 2. Logical Data Model
> 60	
> 61	### 2.1 🟡 `autopilot_plans` (REUSA-ESTENDE — FR-CAD-002 / OTD-CAD-003)
> 62	
> 63	Migration única `ADD COLUMN` × 9 + `DROP CONSTRAINT` × 1. **RLS owner-scoped já existente é PRESERVADA** (nenhuma policy recriada). Colunas legadas (`mode`, `interval_days`, `daily_cap`, `is_active`, `next_run_at`, índice parcial due) permanecem intactas — `plan_kind='viral'` continua válido.
> 64	
> 65	| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | Selo | Description |
> 66	|--------|------|----------|---------|------|-------------|
> 67	| `id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | 🟢 legado | Primary key |
> 68	| `user_id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | — | 🟢 legado | Tenant isolation (FK `auth.users.id`); RLS `= auth.uid()` |
> 69	| `is_active` | `boolean` | NOT NULL | `true` | 🟢 legado | **Kill-switch** (reusado como kill por plano; FM-CAD-14) |
> 70	| `next_run_at` | `timestamptz` | NULL | — | 🟢 legado | Próxima execução em **UTC** (FM-CAD-06); alimenta o índice parcial due |
> 71	| `daily_cap` | `int` | NOT NULL | — | 🟢 legado | Cap diário (clone reusado — G4) |
> 72	| `platforms` (CHECK) | `text[]` | — | — | 🟡 **DROP CHECK** | `platforms <@ ARRAY['wordpress','linkedin','twitter']` **removido**; allowlist passa a derivar de `channel_profiles`/`channel_allowlist` |
> 73	| `plan_kind` | `text` | NOT NULL | `'viral'` | 🔴 nova | `CHECK (plan_kind IN ('viral','cadence'))` — o tick roteia por esta coluna (FR-CAD-004) |
> 74	| `recurrence` | `jsonb` | NULL | — | 🔴 nova | `{frequency:'daily'\|'weekly'\|'monthly', days:int[], hours:int, minutes:int (múltiplo de 5), tz:IANA}` — computado em UTC (FR-CAD-007) |
> 75	| `quiet_hours` | `jsonb` | NULL | — | 🔴 nova | `{start, end, days:int[]}` no fuso do sujeito (FR-CAD-008 · G3) |
> 76	| `overlap_policy` | `text` | NULL | `'skip'` | 🔴 nova | Ciclo anterior rodando ⇒ pula, não empilha (FM-CAD-01) |
> 77	| `catchup_window` | `interval` | NULL | `'6 hours'` | 🔴 nova | Limita backlog após queda (FM-CAD-07) |
> 78	| `jitter_seconds` | `int` | NULL | `0` | 🔴 nova | Anti-thundering-herd no fan-out (FM-CAD-07) |
> 79	| `program` | `jsonb` | NULL | — | 🔴 nova | Steps `publish\|wait\|throttle\|digest\|branch` — **molde `sequences.steps` + `current_step`** (não formato novo — P1/P6) |
> 80	| `channel_allowlist` | `text[]` | NULL | — | 🔴 nova | Canais **por plano** (⊆ allowlist do `auto-publish` **MENOS X** na Fatia 1 — OTD-CAD-011); não constante hardcoded |
> 81	| `budget_cap_mco` | `int` | NOT NULL | `0` | 🔴 nova | Cap **em mcoCoins** (nunca USD); `cadence` exige `> 0` no edge (422) — obrigação compensatória do quote=0 (FR-CAD-011) |
> 82	
> 83	### 2.2 🔴 `cadence_dispatches` (NOVO — FR-CAD-003 / FM-CAD-01)
> 84	
> 85	Ledger por tentativa. **Não é append-only puro** (decisão selada OTD-CAD-006 = retry via índice parcial): `status` transiciona ⇒ exige `UPDATE` service-role. FK composta tenant-escopada para `autopilot_plans`; RLS `select_own`, escrita **service-role only**.

> 89	| `id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | — | Primary key |
> 90	| `user_id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | — | `auth.users.id` | Tenant isolation; RLS `= auth.uid()` (só SELECT) |
> 91	| `plan_id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | — | `autopilot_plans.id` (composta `(plan_id,user_id)` — ver §3) | Plano de origem |

> 93	| `occurrence_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | — | — | Instante vencido (chave de idempotência) |
> 94	| `idempotency_key` | `text` | NOT NULL | — | — | Hash estável de `(plan_id, step_index, occurrence_at)`; sob **índice único parcial** |
> 95	| `channel` | `text` | NOT NULL | — | — | Canal de dispatch (∈ `channel_allowlist` do plano) |
> 96	| `status` | `text` | NOT NULL | `'queued'` | — | `queued\|publishing\|sent\|failed` (**transiciona** — não append-only) |
> 97	| `target_ref` | `text` | NULL | — | — | `scheduled_posts.id` ou id externo (1:1 lógico com o sink) |

> 101	| `created_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | `now()` | — | Audit |
> 102	| `updated_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | `now()` | — | Audit (transição de status) |

> 160	  id                uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
> 161	  user_id           uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

> 183	  ON public.cadence_dispatches (idempotency_key)

> 194	  ON public.cadence_dispatches FOR SELECT
> 195	  USING (user_id = auth.uid());

> 204	### 3.3 🟡 `20260716120200_erase_lead_cascade_cadence.sql` (FR-CAD-017 / G9 / OTD-CAD-006)
> 205	
> 206	```sql
> 207	-- Estender erase_lead() p/ cancelar agendamentos futuros NA MESMA TRANSAÇÃO (LGPD Art. 16/18 VI).
> 208	-- SECURITY DEFINER, search_path='', tenant-guarded — mantém o padrão existente.
> 209	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.erase_lead(p_lead uuid)
> 210	RETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $$
> 211	BEGIN
> 212	  -- ... (bloco existente de anonimização/eliminação do lead) ...
> 213	  UPDATE public.scheduled_posts SET status = 'cancelled'
> 214	    WHERE lead_id = p_lead AND status = 'pending';
> 215	  UPDATE public.cadence_dispatches SET status = 'failed', error = 'erased'
> 216	    WHERE status IN ('queued','publishing')
> 217	      AND user_id = (SELECT user_id FROM public.leads WHERE id = p_lead);
> 218	  -- Atomicidade: rollback se qualquer passo falhar. Sem isso o direito de eliminação é ficção.
> 219	END $$;
> 220	```
> 221	
> 222	### 3.4 🟡 `20260716120300_auto_publish_pgcron_skiplocked.sql` (FR-CAD-018 / OTD-CAD-004)
> 223	
> 224	```sql
> 225	-- Migrar auto-publish de crontab de SO → pg_cron; drain com FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED.
> 226	-- Pré-requisito declarado ("antes de ligar recorrência") — Fatia 0 item 5, não pergunta.
> 227	SELECT cron.schedule('auto-publish', '*/5 * * * *', $$ /* invoke auto-publish edge */ $$);
> 228	-- Claim query no edge:
> 229	--   SELECT ... FROM scheduled_posts WHERE status='pending' AND scheduled_at <= now()
> 230	--     FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED LIMIT <batch>;
> 231	-- NENHUM job pg_cron NOVO para a cadência: o tick autopilot-cadence-cron já existe e apenas roteia.
> 232	```

> 246	
> 247	## 4. GraphRAG Knowledge Nodes
> 248	
> 249	### Node Types Introduced
> 250	
> 251	| node_type | Title Pattern | Content Fields | Embedding Source |
> 252	|-----------|--------------|----------------|-----------------|
> 253	| `observation` (🟢 reuso) | `Cadence cycle · plan <plan_id> · <channel>` | `plan_kind`, canais, `budget_cap_mco`, resultado do 1º ciclo | `embed-mcorch-node` (título + resumo do ciclo) |
> 254	
> 255	> Nenhum `node_type` novo. A Cadência **consome** a Knowledge Mesh (Mesh Connection Mandate) inserindo **um nó de observação por ciclo bem-sucedido** de cada plano — não cria taxonomia nova (P8/P14 n-a de RAG).
> 256	
> 257	### Edge Types Introduced
> 258	
> 259	| Edge relationship | Source node_type | Target node_type | Metadata |
> 260	|-------------------|-----------------|-----------------|----------|
> 261	| `DERIVES_FROM` (🟢 reuso) | `observation` (ciclo) | `creative_asset`/`pillar` a montante | `plan_id`, `channel`, `cycle_at` |
> 262	
> 263	### Embedding Trigger
> 264	
> 265	Toda inserção de nó de observação chama `embed-mcorch-node` (system key `MESH_EMBED_*`, não BYOK per-user — é fluxo de sistema sem `auth.uid()`):
> 266	
> 267	```typescript
> 268	// cadence-run, após o 1º ciclo bem-sucedido do plano:
> 269	await supabase.functions.invoke('embed-mcorch-node', {
> 270	  body: { node_id: observationNode.id, content: `Cadence ${planKind} ${channel} ${summary}` }
> 271	})
> 272	```
> 273	
> 274	### FSRS-6 Parameters
> 275	
> 276	| Field | Initial Value | Decay / Update Rule |
> 277	|-------|--------------|---------------------|
> 278	| `stability_score` | `0.5` | Decai pela fórmula da migration `20260425190000` |
> 279	| `revenue_impact` | Derivado do `collective_efficiency_ledger` do ciclo (mco/resultado) | Atualizado a cada ciclo reconciliado (custo BYOK **medido** mesmo com mco=0 — NFR-CAD-008) |
> 280	
> 281	---
> 282	
> 283	## 5. Tenant Isolation Map
> 284	
> 285	| Table | Selo | `user_id` scope | FK composta tenant-escopada | Escrita do cliente | System rows |
> 286	|-------|------|-----------------|-----------------------------|--------------------|-------------|
> 287	| `autopilot_plans` | 🟡 | ✅ Required (RLS `= auth.uid()` **preservada**) | é o **alvo** de `(id,user_id)` | Via `cadence-plan` (edge, JWT) | Não |
> 288	| `cadence_dispatches` | 🔴 | ✅ Required (RLS `select_own`) | `(plan_id,user_id) → autopilot_plans(id,user_id)` | **NUNCA** (escrita só service-role) | Não |
> 289	| `scheduled_posts` | 🟢 | ✅ Required (RLS existente) | via `lead_id`/owner | Via edge (contrato reshape) | Não |
> 290	| `lead_events` | 🟢 | ✅ Required (imutável: só select_own + insert_own) | `(id,user_id)` existente | Insert-own | Não |
> 291	| `social_threads`/`_messages` | 🔒 | ✅ Required (`select_own`) | `inbound_event_id → lead_events`; composta como acima | **NUNCA** (service-role) | Não |
> 292	| `channel_profiles.cadence` | 🟢 | ✅ Required (existente) | — | Não (só leitura pelo motor) | Não |
> 293	| `profiles.timezone` | 🟢 | ✅ Required (existente) | — | Via Settings (existente) | Não |
> 294	| `mcorch_nodes` | 🟢 | Optional | `user_id + project_id` | Service-role (observação) | ✅ (system `user_id IS NULL`) |
> 295	
> 296	> **Regra de tenancy (SDD §5.1 STRIDE):** `user_id` **jamais** vem do body — na cadência ele é **server-trusted da LINHA** de `autopilot_plans` drenada pelo tick (SOP `autopilot-cron-identity.md`), e no `cadence-plan` vem da **claim ES256 verificada** (nunca `atob` cego). A FK composta `(plan_id,user_id)` impede que um dispatch aponte para um plano de outro tenant mesmo se o `plan_id` for adivinhado.
> 297

> 300	## 6. Data Quality Rules
> 301	
> 302	| Table | Column | Rule | Enforcement | Violation Message (PT-BR) |
> 303	|-------|--------|------|-------------|---------------------------|
> 304	| `autopilot_plans` | `plan_kind` | `IN ('viral','cadence')` | CHECK | — (interno) |
> 305	| `autopilot_plans` | `budget_cap_mco` | NOT NULL; **`> 0` quando `plan_kind='cadence'`** | CHECK/edge 422 | "Defina um teto de orçamento em mcoCoins para a cadência." |
> 306	| `autopilot_plans` | `recurrence.minutes` | Múltiplo de 5 (semântica Knock) | edge 422 | "Os minutos devem ser múltiplos de 5." |
> 307	| `autopilot_plans` | `channel_allowlist` | ⊆ allowlist do `auto-publish` **MENOS X** (Fatia 1) | edge 422 | "Canal não disponível nesta cadência." |
> 308	| `cadence_dispatches` | `idempotency_key` | Único **enquanto `status <> 'failed'`** (falha libera, sucesso prende) | UNIQUE INDEX parcial | — (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING; anti double-post FM-CAD-01) |
> 309	| `cadence_dispatches` | `status` | `sent` **só** a partir do webhook de status, nunca do 202 | lógica de reconcile | — (Lei 1 / FM-CAD-05) |
> 310	| `cadence_dispatches` | A/B bucket | `mod(abs(hashtext(subject‖':'‖exp)::bigint),100) < ratio` — cast `bigint` **antes** do `abs` | fórmula SQL literal | — (FR-CAD-010; `% 100` com sinal enviesa em silêncio) |
> 311	| `social_threads` | `inbound_event_id` | **NOT NULL** (FK a `lead_events` message_received) | FK NOT NULL | — (cold DM impossível de representar — G1) |
> 312	| `social_threads` | `comment_id` | **UNIQUE** (1 private reply por comentário) | UNIQUE | — (G2; retry duplicado = violação de política) |
> 313	| `marketing_consents` | `basis` | `= 'consent'` fail-closed no Brasil (até Guia ANPD literal) | gate de enqueue | "Consentimento necessário para este canal." (OTD-CAD-015) |
> 314	| `marketing_consents` | `granted_at`+`source_proof` | NOT NULL p/ enqueue WhatsApp | edge 422 | `whatsapp_opt_in_missing` (FM-CAD-15) |
> 315	| (jurisdição G11) | país do lead | Ausente ⇒ tratar como **UE opt-in prévio** (fail-closed) | gate de send | — (custa conversão, é seguro) |
> 316	
> 317	---
> 318	
> 319	## 7. Retention & Archival Policy
> 320	
> 321	| Data | Retention | Archival Strategy | Deletion Trigger |
> 322	|------|-----------|-------------------|------------------|
> 323	| `autopilot_plans` (cadence) | Enquanto ativo | — | `erase_lead()` cascade + cancelamento manual |
> 324	| `cadence_dispatches` | Ledger histórico (auditoria de reconcile/custo) | — | `ON DELETE CASCADE` do plano; `status='failed', error='erased'` no `erase_lead()` (LGPD Art. 16/18 VI — G9) |
> 325	| `scheduled_posts` pendentes | Até publicar/cancelar | drenado por `auto-publish` (pg_cron + SKIP LOCKED) | `status='cancelled'` no `erase_lead()` cascade |
> 326	| `social_threads`/`_messages` `[PROBE-GATED]` | Histórico local (Meta só devolve 20 últimas) | — | Cascade do lead; `erase_lead()` |
> 327	| `lead_events` | Append-only imutável | — | Somente via `erase_lead()` tenant-guarded |
> 328	| `infra_health_logs` (`service='spaces-cadence'`) | 90 dias | Sem archival | Cron purge |
> 329	| `mcorch_nodes` (observação) | Indefinido | Backfill `scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts` | Com a linha pai |
> 330	
> 331	> **Direito de eliminação é estrutural (não best-effort):** `erase_lead()` cancela `scheduled_posts` + `cadence_dispatches` pendentes **na mesma transação** (§3.3). Sem essa cascata o LGPD Art. 16/18 VI vira ficção (G9/OTD-CAD-006) — a revogação chegando **depois** do enqueue ainda impede o send (`withdrawn_at` checado no send, independente da base legal — FM-CAD-04).
> 332

> 335	## 8. Traceability Matrix (Data ↔ FR ↔ OTD ↔ FM)
> 336	
> 337	| Objeto de dado | Selo | FR-CAD | OTD-CAD | FM-CAD | NFR-CAD |
> 338	|----------------|------|--------|---------|--------|---------|
> 339	| `autopilot_plans` (+9 col, −1 CHECK) | 🟡 | 002, 004, 007 | 003 | 06 | 006, 012 |

> 1	# Quality & Self-Optimization Specification
> 2	
> 3	**Project:** spaces-cadence
> 4	**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-07-15
> 5	**BoK Applied:** CMQ/OE BOK (Process Management + Organizational Excellence) — anti-Goodhart, medição material por execução, ligação ao Collective Efficiency Ledger
> 6	**Status:** Draft
> 7	**Source of truth:** [`00-deepsearch-blueprint.md`](00-deepsearch-blueprint.md) (verificação externa 2026-07-14) + [`01-mrd.md`](01-mrd.md) → [`05-sdd.md`](05-sdd.md) + [`06-data-model.md`](06-data-model.md). Todo KPI/FMEA/gate abaixo deriva de um `FR-CAD-xxx` / `NFR-CAD-xxx` / `OTD-CAD-xxx` / `FM-CAD-xx` / guardrail `Gn` já selado; nada é inventado além do blueprint (Lei 1).
> 8	
> 9	> **ORO triplet desta task:**
> 10	> - **Operator:** MCORCH Master Execution Agent
> 11	> - **Reviewer:** Sovereign (Gabriel)
> 12	> - **Owner:** Sovereign — blast radius material: **ban de app Meta/X** (spam de 1 tenant no app global bane todos — OTD-CAD-018) + **sanção ANPD até 2% do faturamento** (LGPD Art. 52).
> 13	>
> 14	> **Nota de fidelidade (Lei 1):** as métricas de inbound (FR-CAD-012/013) permanecem `[PROBE-GATED]` — só entram em vigor após o probe FM-CAD-02 (RPN 486) fechar com DM real de terceiro sem role + payload cru arquivado. §3 do blueprint (posicionamento vs. ManyChat) permanece sob **SELO DE NÃO-VERIFICAÇÃO**: nenhum KPI abaixo cita §3 como fato.
> 15	
> 16	---
> 17	
> 18	## 0. Filosofia de qualidade — o gate anti-Goodhart (CMQ/OE)
> 19	
> 20	> *"When a measure becomes a target, it ceases to be a good measure."* — Goodhart. Em cadência de publicação a lei de Goodhart é **letal**: a métrica ingênua ("posts publicados/dia", "DMs respondidas", "mensagens enviadas") é **exatamente** o que um bot de spam maximiza — e é o que o §6 do blueprint proíbe por construção (G3/G4: frequência excessiva = ban do app).
> 21	
> 22	**Três invariantes anti-Goodhart deste módulo (todos já materializados nos docs 05/06):**
> 23	
> 24	1. **A verdade é financeira, não de volume.** O denominador de todo KPI de eficácia é o `collective_efficiency_ledger` (mco/resultado + overhead, derivado de `pipeline_runs`/`autopilot_cycles` — a verdade financeira, não um contador auto-reportado). Custo externo BYOK que o tenant absorve (Meta AI Provider BR, X post-com-URL) é **medido** em `cadence_dispatches.external_usd_cost` mesmo quando a cobrança em mco é 0 (NFR-CAD-008) — "não cobrar ≠ não medir". **Maximizar volume sem receita PIORA o KPI** (mco/resultado sobe).
> 25	2. **`sent` não é auto-declarado.** Nenhum KPI de entrega conta a partir do `202` da API — só do **webhook de status** (NFR-CAD-002 / FM-CAD-05). Um contador que se incrementa sozinho é uma mentira mensurável (Lei 1).
> 26	3. **Halt é sucesso, não falha.** Quiet-hours HALT (FR-CAD-008), frequency-cap HALT, dedup/digest (FR-CAD-009) e opt-out no send **reduzem** o volume publicado de propósito. O KPI de saúde de cadência **recompensa** a supressão correta (é a diferença entre cadência e flood), nunca a penaliza.
> 27	
> 28	**Regra de leitura:** todo KPI de "quantidade" (KPI-CAD-002/003) só é interpretável **junto** ao seu par de eficiência (KPI-CAD-006/007). Volume isolado é proibido como meta (herda o gap OTD-AG do ledger — sem álgebra de priorização entre planos, o cap pode causar starvation; ver Pattern P20 `deferred` na SDD §9).
> 29	
> 30	---
> 31	
> 32	## 1. Quality Objectives
> 33	
> 34	| QO ID | Objective | Linked BR / BO | Target | Anti-Goodhart guard | Review Cadence |
> 35	|-------|-----------|----------------|--------|---------------------|----------------|
> 36	| QO-CAD-001 | **Zero double-post** sob tick sobreposto/retry (integridade da idempotência) | BR-CAD-003 · FR-CAD-003 · NFR-CAD-001 | 0 dispatches duplicados por `(plan_id, step_index, occurrence_at)` | Contado no ledger `cadence_dispatches`, não no cliente | Weekly |
> 37	| QO-CAD-002 | **Materialidade da entrega** — `sent` só do webhook de status, nunca do 202 | FR-CAD-006 · NFR-CAD-002 · FM-CAD-05 | 100% dos `sent` reconciliados | Reconcile obrigatório; 202 nunca marca sent | Weekly |

> 108	
> 109	## 5. Self-Healing Specifications
> 110	
> 111	### 5.1 Watchdog Telemetry Events
> 112	
> 113	Novos `event` escritos em `infra_health_logs` (`service='spaces-cadence'`) — allowlist no chokepoint para **não** vazar dado per-tenant na tabela global-read (SDD §6 / NFR-CAD-015):
> 114	
> 115	| Event | Trigger | Severity | Payload (allowlisted) |
> 116	|-------|---------|----------|-----------------------|
> 117	| `cadence_dispatch` | Dispatch enfileirado em `scheduled_posts` | info | `{plan_id, step_index, channel, occurrence_at}` |
> 118	| `cadence_halt_quiet_hours` | Dispatch suprimido por quiet-hours (fuso do sujeito) | info | `{plan_id, channel, tz}` |
> 119	| `cadence_halt_freq_cap` | HALT por frequency-cap (teto `channel_profiles.cadence`) | info | `{plan_id, channel, window}` |
> 120	| `cadence_reconcile` | `sent` confirmado pelo webhook de status | info | `{dispatch_id, external_usd_cost, cost_source}` |
> 121	| `cadence_fn_failure` | `cadence-run`/`cadence-plan` 5xx após retry | error | `{fn_name, error_message}` (nunca corpo per-tenant) |
> 122	| `template_recategorized` | `message_template_status_update` (utility→marketing) | warn | `{template_id, old_cat, new_cat}` |
> 123	| `kill_switch_tripped` | Feedback negativo acima do limiar (por-app) `[PROBE-GATED]` | error | `{app_scope, ratio_estimate}` |
> 124	
> 125	### 5.2 Auto-Recovery Script Stub
> 126	
> 127	```bash
> 128	#!/bin/bash
> 129	# scripts/self-heal-spaces-cadence.sh
> 130	# Triggered by mega-brain-daemon when cadence_fn_failure detected.
> 131	# NUNCA reprocessa dispatch já 'sent' (idempotência via índice parcial garante no-double-post).
> 132	
> 133	FUNCTION_NAME="cadence-run"
> 134	echo "[$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)] Self-heal for $FUNCTION_NAME"
> 135	
> 136	# Step 1: health-check da edge fn (cron-only → Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY)
> 137	STATUS=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" \
> 138	  "$SUPABASE_URL/functions/v1/$FUNCTION_NAME/health" \
> 139	  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SB_SECRET_KEY")
> 140	if [ "$STATUS" != "200" ]; then
> 141	  echo "[WARN] unhealthy ($STATUS). Re-deploying..."
> 142	  npx supabase functions deploy "$FUNCTION_NAME"
> 143	fi
> 144	
> 145	# Step 2: NÃO re-armar planos com dispatch pendente de reconcile — deixar o webhook de status resolver.
> 146	#         (Reconciliação é a fonte da verdade de 'sent'; forçar re-run = risco de double-post.)
> 147	echo "[OK] Self-heal complete (reconcile deixado para o webhook de status — FM-CAD-05)."
> 148	```
> 149	
> 150	### 5.3 mega-brain-daemon Integration Points
> 151	
> 152	| Daemon Event | Condition | Action |
> 153	|--------------|-----------|--------|
> 154	| `health_check` | A cada 5 min (watchdog cron) | Comparar `count(*) FROM cron.job` vs. baseline (**deve ser invariante** — NFR-CAD-012) |
> 155	| `anomaly_detected` | `infra_health_logs` `cadence_fn_failure` rate > 5/h | Executar `self-heal-spaces-cadence.sh` |
> 156	| `integrity_breach` | KPI-CAD-001 double-post > 0 **ou** KPI-CAD-010 compliance > 0 | Pausar re-arm do plano + `notify()` Sovereign (não auto-corrige compliance — decisão humana) |
> 157	| `recovery_confirmed` | Health-check passa após self-heal | Inserir `recovery_ok` em `infra_health_logs` |
> 158

> 161	## 6. SLA / SLO / SLI Definitions
> 162	
> 163	| Service | SLI (o que medimos) | SLO (nosso alvo) | SLA (consequência) |
> 164	|---------|---------------------|------------------|--------------------|
> 165	| `cadence-plan` API | p95 latência de arm | ≤ 800 ms | Investigação se > 2 s por > 1% das chamadas/mês |
> 166	| `cadence-run` (por tick) | p95 por batch de 50, fan-out bounded | < 5 s (NFR-CAD-013) | Incident report se > 10 s recorrente |
> 167	| Idempotência de dispatch | double-post rate (KPI-CAD-001) | **0** | Halt do plano + auditoria Lei 1 se > 0 |
> 168	| Reconcile de entrega | % `sent` reconciliado do webhook (KPI-CAD-004) | ≥ 95% em ≤ 10 min | Re-poll automático; alerta se webhook mudo > 6h |
> 169	| Infra (contenção) | jobs pg_cron adicionados | **+0** (NFR-CAD-012) | Bloqueia release (viola mandato de integração) |
> 170	| Isolamento tenant | count cross-tenant (`cadence_dispatches`/`autopilot_plans`) | **0** | `/security-review` NO FINDINGS obrigatório antes do commit |
> 171	| Custo BYOK visível | % dispatch BYOK com `external_usd_cost` (KPI-CAD-007) | 100% | Bloquear agregação ao ledger até reconciliar (NFR-CAD-008) |

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	#!/usr/bin/env bun
> 2	/**
> 3	 * scripts/video-repurpose/segment-core.ts
> 4	 *
> 5	 * The FFmpeg core of the video-repurpose engine (Fatia 2): a long 16:9 master + a data-driven cut-spec →
> 6	 * N vertical shorts (9:16 or 1:1), reframed subject-safe (center-crop) with an optional burned caption.
> 7	 *
> 8	 * BoK SSOT: docs/bok/video-repurpose/00-deepsearch-blueprint.md §Pilar II (FR-VR-003/004/005).
> 9	 * PROVEN offline 2026-07-12 (Vision QA): a synthetic 16:9 master with ESQ|CENTRO|DIR labels → the 9:16 center-crop
> 10	 * kept CENTRO, dropped the side labels, and burned the caption at the base. Deterministic FFmpeg — no LLM in the core.
> 11	 *
> 12	 * Reframe is source-agnostic (expression crop): for a target aspect ar, keep the largest centered rect of that aspect.
> 13	 * Caption is written to a per-clip textfile → drawtext `textfile=` (avoids the `:'%\` escaping trap of inline text).
> 14	 * Cut is re-encoded (`-ss <in> -i -t <dur>`) → frame-accurate (the reframe re-encodes anyway).
> 15	 */
> 16	
> 17	import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
> 18	import { writeFileSync, mkdirSync, statSync, existsSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
> 19	import { join } from 'node:path';
> 20	import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
> 21	import { renderAlphaFrames } from '../hyperframes/render-core.ts';
> 22	
> 23	const FONT = '/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf';
> 24	
> 25	export type Reframe = '9:16' | '1:1';
> 26	export interface TextBeat { t_start: number; t_end: number; phrase: string; emphasis?: 'hook' | 'punch' | 'normal' }
> 27	export interface ClipSpec {
> 28	  in_sec: number;
> 29	  out_sec: number;
> 30	  caption?: string;          // burned bottom-center caption (drawtext textfile); omit for none
> 31	  reframe?: Reframe;         // default '9:16'
> 32	  // FR-VR-011/014: 'beats' = punctual motion-graphic overlay (viral-caption-overlay-9x16); default legacy drawtext.
> 33	  caption_mode?: 'beats' | 'drawtext' | 'none';
> 34	  text_beats?: TextBeat[];   // clip-relative beats for caption_mode='beats'
> 35	}
> 36	export interface SegmentSpec {
> 37	  sourcePath: string;        // local path to the master MP4
> 38	  clips: ClipSpec[];
> 39	  outDir: string;
> 40	  fps?: number;              // default 30 (CFR)
> 41	}
> 42	export interface ClipResult {
> 43	  index: number; path: string; width: number; height: number; durationSec: number; bytes: number;
> 44	}
> 45	
> 46	const TARGET: Record<Reframe, { w: number; h: number; ar: number }> = {
> 47	  '9:16': { w: 1080, h: 1920, ar: 9 / 16 },
> 48	  '1:1': { w: 1080, h: 1080, ar: 1 },
> 49	};
> 50	
> 51	function run(cmd: string, args: string[]): Promise<void> {
> 52	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
> 53	    const p = spawn(cmd, args, { stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });
> 54	    let err = '';
> 55	    p.stderr.on('data', (d) => { err += d.toString(); });
> 56	    p.on('error', reject);
> 57	    p.on('close', (code) => code === 0 ? resolve() : reject(new Error(`${cmd} exit ${code}: ${err.slice(-500)}`)));
> 58	  });
> 59	}
> 60	
> 61	/** Source-agnostic center-crop to `ar`, then scale to (W,H). Keeps the largest centered rect of the target aspect. */
> 62	function reframeChain(ar: number, W: number, H: number): string {
> 63	  // crop w = min(iw, ih*ar); h = min(ih, iw/ar); centered. Expressions are evaluated by FFmpeg per input dims.
> 64	  const cw = `min(iw\\,ih*${ar})`;
> 65	  const ch = `min(ih\\,iw/${ar})`;
> 66	  return `crop=${cw}:${ch}:(iw-ow)/2:(ih-oh)/2,scale=${W}:${H}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=${W}:${H},setsar=1`;
> 67	}
> 68	
> 69	/** Cut + reframe + optional caption for ONE clip. Returns the output path. */
> 70	async function renderClip(spec: SegmentSpec, clip: ClipSpec, index: number): Promise<ClipResult> {
> 71	  const target = TARGET[clip.reframe ?? '9:16'];
> 72	  const fps = spec.fps ?? 30;
> 73	  const dur = Math.max(0.1, clip.out_sec - clip.in_sec);
> 74	  const outPath = join(spec.outDir, `clip_${String(index).padStart(3, '0')}.mp4`);
> 75	  const reframe = reframeChain(target.ar, target.w, target.h);
> 76	
> 77	  // FR-VR-011: motion-graphic "text beats" — render the transparent (RGBA) text layer, composite OVER the
> 78	  // reframed footage in a single pass (reframe [0:v] → overlay the alpha PNG seq [1:v]). Not the drawtext bar.
> 79	  if (clip.caption_mode === 'beats' && clip.text_beats && clip.text_beats.length) {
> 80	    const alphaDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'vr-beats-'));
> 81	    try {
> 82	      await renderAlphaFrames(
> 83	        { template: 'viral-caption-overlay-9x16', props: { beats: clip.text_beats }, duration_s: dur, fps, width: target.w, height: target.h },
> 84	        alphaDir,
> 85	      );
> 86	      await run('ffmpeg', [
> 87	        '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error',
> 88	        // `-ss`+`-t` are INPUT options for input 0 (the master) — MUST precede its `-i`, else `-t` binds to the
> 89	        // next input (the PNG seq) and the master plays to EOF (→ a ~500s file). eof_action=pass then harmless.
> 90	        '-ss', String(clip.in_sec), '-t', String(dur), '-i', spec.sourcePath,
> 91	        '-framerate', String(fps), '-i', join(alphaDir, 'f_%05d.png'),
> 92	        '-filter_complex', `[0:v]${reframe}[bg];[bg][1:v]overlay=0:0:format=auto:eof_action=pass[v]`,
> 93	        '-map', '[v]', '-map', '0:a?', '-r', String(fps),
> 94	        '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'medium', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p',
> 95	        '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '128k', '-movflags', '+faststart',
> 96	        outPath,
> 97	      ]);
> 98	    } finally {
> 99	      rmSync(alphaDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
> 100	    }
> 101	    const bytes = statSync(outPath).size;
> 102	    return { index, path: outPath, width: target.w, height: target.h, durationSec: dur, bytes };
> 103	  }
> 104	
> 105	  // Legacy path: reframe + optional burned drawtext caption (caption_mode 'drawtext'/undefined).
> 106	  let vf = reframe;
> 107	  if (clip.caption && clip.caption.trim() && clip.caption_mode !== 'none') {
> 108	    // textfile avoids inline-escaping; drawtext reads UTF-8. Bottom-center with a translucent box.
> 109	    const capPath = join(spec.outDir, `cap_${index}.txt`);
> 110	    // drawtext(textfile) in this ffmpeg build truncates the rendered text by the number of EXTRA UTF-8 bytes
> 111	    // (byteLen − codepointLen) — a byte-vs-char bug that clips accented captions' tails ("notícia." → "notíc").
> 112	    // Pad with that many trailing spaces so the truncation eats the padding, not the caption (proven via Vision QA).
> 113	    const cap = clip.caption.trim();
> 114	    const capPad = Buffer.byteLength(cap, 'utf8') - [...cap].length;
> 115	    writeFileSync(capPath, cap + ' '.repeat(Math.max(0, capPad)));
> 116	    const fontsize = Math.round(target.w * 0.045);
> 117	    vf += `,drawtext=textfile='${capPath}':fontfile=${FONT}:fontsize=${fontsize}:fontcolor=white:` +
> 118	      `box=1:boxcolor=black@0.55:boxborderw=14:x=(w-tw)/2:y=h-th-${Math.round(target.h * 0.08)}`;
> 119	  }
> 120	
> 121	  await run('ffmpeg', [
> 122	    '-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error',
> 123	    '-ss', String(clip.in_sec), '-i', spec.sourcePath, '-t', String(dur),
> 124	    '-vf', vf, '-r', String(fps),
> 125	    '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'medium', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p',
> 126	    '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '128k', '-movflags', '+faststart',
> 127	    outPath,
> 128	  ]);
> 129	
> 130	  const bytes = statSync(outPath).size;
> 131	  return { index, path: outPath, width: target.w, height: target.h, durationSec: dur, bytes };
> 132	}
> 133	
> 134	/** Segment a master into N clips per the cut-spec. Serial (deterministic, memory-safe for a long master). */
> 135	export async function segmentVideo(spec: SegmentSpec): Promise<ClipResult[]> {
> 136	  if (!existsSync(spec.sourcePath)) throw new Error(`source_not_found: ${spec.sourcePath}`);
> 137	  if (!Array.isArray(spec.clips) || spec.clips.length === 0) throw new Error('cut_spec_empty');
> 138	  mkdirSync(spec.outDir, { recursive: true });
> 139	  const out: ClipResult[] = [];
> 140	  for (let i = 0; i < spec.clips.length; i++) {
> 141	    const c = spec.clips[i];
> 142	    if (!(c.out_sec > c.in_sec) || c.in_sec < 0) throw new Error(`clip_${i}_invalid_range`);
> 143	    out.push(await renderClip(spec, c, i));
> 144	  }
> 145	  return out;
> 146	}
> 147	
> ```

> ```
> 1	# SOP — Provisionamento da infra host do Video Repurpose (Lei 2)
> 2	
> 3	> **Por que existe:** o motor de repurpose usa infra que vive **fora do container Supabase e fora do build do frontend** — dois workers systemd + um receiver de upload atrás do nginx. Antes desta SOP, esses artefatos existiam só no host (`~/.config/systemd/user/`, `/etc/nginx`), **não versionados** → risco de perda silenciosa num reprovisionamento. Esta SOP + os arquivos em `infra/systemd/` e `infra/nginx/` fecham o alerta "infra host fora do git".
> 4	>
> 5	> **ORO:** Operator = Sovereign (host root/user `ubuntu`) · Reviewer = Sovereign · Owner = Sovereign (blast radius = disco do host + porta loopback; rail grátis US$ 0).
> 6	> **Materialidade (Lei 1):** as 2 units foram capturadas **verbatim** do host (`~/.config/systemd/user/`). O bloco nginx `/api/host-upload` **não pôde ser lido** no host (rodando como `ubuntu`, `/etc/nginx` é permission-denied; `nginx -T` não permitido) → `infra/nginx/host-upload.location.conf` é a **forma REQUERIDA derivada** de `scripts/host-upload-server.ts`; o provisionamento reconcilia contra o bloco vivo.
> 7	
> 8	## Artefatos versionados (fonte da verdade no git)
> 9	
> 10	| Artefato | Repo (versionado) | Host (deployado) |
> 11	|---|---|---|
> 12	| Worker de segmentação | `scripts/video-repurpose-bridge.ts` | roda via unit abaixo |
> 13	| Receiver de upload | `scripts/host-upload-server.ts` | roda via unit abaixo (`127.0.0.1:3220`) |
> 14	| Unit — bridge | `infra/systemd/video-repurpose-bridge.service` | `~/.config/systemd/user/video-repurpose-bridge.service` |
> 15	| Unit — upload | `infra/systemd/host-upload.service` | `~/.config/systemd/user/host-upload.service` |
> 16	| Location nginx | `infra/nginx/host-upload.location.conf` (derivado) | `/etc/nginx/sites-enabled/*login.mcorch.com*` (não-repo-legível) |
> 17	| Inbox no disco | — | `<repo>/repurpose-inbox/<uid>/` (gitignored; dados) |
> 18	
> 19	## Operator — quem executa
> 20	O Sovereign no host (usuário `ubuntu` para systemd `--user`; root/sudo para o nginx).
> 21	
> 22	## Sequence — ordem (cada passo com critério material)
> 23	
> 24	1. **Instalar as units** (idempotente — os arquivos já existem no host; este passo re-sincroniza a partir do git):
> 25	   ```bash
> 26	   cp infra/systemd/host-upload.service infra/systemd/video-repurpose-bridge.service ~/.config/systemd/user/
> 27	   systemctl --user daemon-reload
> 28	   systemctl --user enable --now host-upload.service video-repurpose-bridge.service
> 29	   ```
> 30	   ✅ sucesso: `systemctl --user is-active host-upload.service video-repurpose-bridge.service` → `active active`.
> 31	
> 32	2. **Reconciliar o nginx** (root): abrir o server block `server_name login.mcorch.com` e garantir que o `location = /api/host-upload` bate com `infra/nginx/host-upload.location.conf` (mesmos `proxy_pass 127.0.0.1:3220`, `proxy_request_buffering off`, `client_max_body_size 100m`, timeouts 300s). Se divergir, **atualizar o git** (Lei 1 — o repo segue a verdade do host) OU aplicar o bloco versionado ao host, e registrar qual venceu.
> 33	   ```bash
> 34	   sudo nginx -t && sudo systemctl reload nginx
> 35	   ```
> 36	   ✅ sucesso: `nginx -t` → `syntax is ok / test is successful`.
> 37	
> 38	3. **Inbox no disco**: `mkdir -p <repo>/repurpose-inbox` (o server cria `<uid>/` sob demanda; realpath-contido).
> 39	
> 40	## Verification gates (o operator confirma cada um materialmente)
> 41	
> 42	| Gate | Comando | Esperado |
> 43	|---|---|---|
> 44	| G1 units ativas | `systemctl --user is-active host-upload.service video-repurpose-bridge.service` | `active` (×2) |
> 45	| G2 porta loopback | `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X POST http://127.0.0.1:3220/api/host-upload` | `401` (sem JWT) — prova que sobe e exige auth |
> 46	| G3 admin-gate | POST com JWT de user **não-admin** | `403 admin_only` |
> 47	| G4 nginx front-door | `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X POST https://login.mcorch.com/api/host-upload` | `401` (chega no server, exige auth) — **não** 404/502 |
> 48	| G5 telemetria | `SELECT count(*) FROM infra_health_logs WHERE service='host-upload' AND created_at > now()-interval '1 day'` | ≥1 no primeiro upload real |
> 49	
> 50	## Recovery — falha no passo N
> 51	- **Unit em crash-loop** (`Restart=always` + `StartLimitBurst=5/60s`): `journalctl --user -u host-upload.service -n 50` → causa (falta `.env`? bun no PATH?). O unit já fixa `PATH=/home/ubuntu/.bun/bin:…` e carrega `.env` de `../` (o server faz isso). Corrigir e `systemctl --user restart <unit>`.
> 52	- **G4 = 502**: o server loopback caiu → G1/G2 primeiro. **G4 = 404**: o `location` não está no server block → passo 2. **G4 = 403 do Cloudflare** (não do server): challenge de IP datacenter → mesmo playbook `docs/processes/wordpress-cf-publish-unblock.md` (Security Level / WAF Skip).
> 53	- **Upload trava em chunk grande**: confirmar `proxy_request_buffering off` + `client_max_body_size 100m` no bloco vivo (sem isso, nginx bufferiza e estoura). CF corta >100MB/request → o cliente DEVE fatiar <100MB (já fatia ~80MB).
> 54	
> 55	## Rota de mídia `/api/host-media` (2026-07-13 — master reproduzível na biblioteca)
> 56	
> 57	**Por quê:** o master host-local é `creative_assets` `storage_bucket='local'` — a biblioteca tentava assinar no Supabase Storage → `Object not found` → player quebrado ("o arquivo corrompeu" — não corrompeu; faltava rota de reprodução).
> 58	
> 59	**Desenho:** `host-upload-server` ganhou `GET /api/host-media?key=<uid>/<file>` — mesmos guards do upload (JWT `admin.getUser` + admin-gate `user_roles` + owner-scope `key.startsWith(uid/)` + containment em `INBOX_BASE` + `..` banido) + **streaming com Range** (HTTP 206 → seek). `<video src>` não envia headers → JWT curto da sessão vai como `?token=` (same-origin TLS, admin-only; o `useDisplayUrl` re-resolve antes de expirar; token em access-log do próprio host = aceito e documentado). Cliente: `src/lib/asset-url.ts` branch `bucket==='local'`. `/security-review` **NO FINDINGS** (traversal/auth/cross-tenant/header-injection verificados).
> 60	
> 61	**Apply (ação Sovereign):** `sudo bash scripts/qa/apply-host-media-nginx.sh` (idempotente: backup → insere a location após o bloco host-upload → `nginx -t` → reload).
> 62	
> 63	**Gates:** G6 local `curl -H 'Range: bytes=0-1023' 'http://127.0.0.1:3220/api/host-media?key=<uid>/<file>&token=<jwt>'` → `206` + `Content-Range` correto (provado 2026-07-13 com o EP01 de 1.336.271.927 bytes; seek em 600MB ok; cross-tenant → 400; sem token → 401; não-admin → 403). G7 público (pós-apply): mesmo curl via `https://login.mcorch.com` → 206; biblioteca reproduz o master.
> 64	
> 65	## Success signal (materialmente observável)
> 66	Um upload real (admin, via UI `/dashboard/repurpose`) grava `repurpose-inbox/<uid>/<file>.mp4` no disco (`ls -la` com size ≈ o master) **e** o `ingest-external-asset provider=local` registra o `creative_assets bucket=local`, consumível pelo `video-repurpose-run`. Prova no seal 2026-07-13: EP01 1,3 GB → 5 shorts.
> 67	
> 68	**Cross-links:** [[project_video_repurpose_engine]] · `docs/bok/video-repurpose/00-deepsearch-blueprint.md` (OTD-VR-001 yt-dlp datacenter-block) · `docs/processes/build-deploy-materiality.md`.
> 69	
> ```

> #!/usr/bin/env bash
> # guard-sweep.sh — consolidated PRODUCTION REGRESSION battery for MCORCH.
> # Runs every re-executable guard (sentinels, hermetic smokes, drift/IoC/doctor) against the LIVE
> # environment and fails if ANY of them fails. Sibling of .claude/scripts/mcorch-doctor.sh:
> # doctor = consolidated DIAGNOSIS (state inspection) · guard-sweep = consolidated REGRESSION
> # (re-proves that everything shipped still works and is still secure TODAY).
> #
> # Born from the all-green sweep of 2026-07-06 (Workflow wf_1dacd877 — Mandato Obstáculo→Síntese:
> # a practice that proved value becomes a tool). Zero-cost by design: every guard here is hermetic
> # (throwaway users + cleanup) or read-only (422/metadata probes).
> #
> # Usage:  bash scripts/qa/guard-sweep.sh [--fast]
> #   --fast   skip the two slowest guards (mcorch-doctor --deep and schema-drift) for a quick pulse.
> # Exit code = number of failing guards (0 = all green).
> set -uo pipefail
> cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.."
> 
> # Cron-safe PATH (incident INC-20260707T1030Z-96643584: under cron, `bun`/`npx` are not on PATH →
> # 6/7 guards failed with 'command not found' — a false RED about the environment, not production).
> export PATH="/home/ubuntu/.bun/bin:/home/ubuntu/.nvm/versions/node/v22.22.3/bin:$PATH"
> 
> FAST=0
> [ "${1:-}" = "--fast" ] && FAST=1
> 
> declare -a NAMES CMDS
> NAMES+=("asset-owner-signing (storage RLS, 6 gates)");      CMDS+=("bun run scripts/qa/smoke-asset-owner-signing.ts")
> NAMES+=("semantic-synapses (RPC guard, 8 gates)");          CMDS+=("bun run scripts/qa/smoke-semantic-synapses.ts")
> NAMES+=("higgsfield-schema (provider sentinel, 7 gates)");  CMDS+=("bun run scripts/qa/smoke-higgsfield-schema.ts")
> NAMES+=("higgsfield-mcp-contract (OAuth sentinel, 11)");    CMDS+=("bun run scripts/qa/smoke-higgsfield-mcp-contract.ts")
> NAMES+=("supply-chain-iocs (IoC scanner)");                 CMDS+=("bun run .claude/scripts/scan-supply-chain-iocs.ts")
> NAMES+=("spaces-execution (money path, 40 gates)");         CMDS+=("bun run scripts/qa/smoke-spaces-execution.ts")
> NAMES+=("spaces-watchdog (ledger recovery, 6 gates)");      CMDS+=("bun run scripts/qa/smoke-spaces-watchdog.ts")
> NAMES+=("youtube-write-ops (force-ssl gate, 9)");           CMDS+=("bun run scripts/qa/smoke-youtube-write-ops.ts")
> NAMES+=("scheduled-carousel (IG carousel, 9 gates)");       CMDS+=("bun run scripts/qa/smoke-scheduled-carousel.ts")
> if [ "$FAST" -eq 0 ]; then
>   NAMES+=("schema-drift (deployed↔migrations)");            CMDS+=("bash scripts/qa/audit-schema-drift.sh")
>   NAMES+=("mcorch-doctor --deep (9 sections)");             CMDS+=("bash .claude/scripts/mcorch-doctor.sh --deep")
> fi
> 
> FAILS=0
> TOTAL=${#NAMES[@]}
> declare -a ROWS
> echo "🛡️  MCORCH guard-sweep — ${TOTAL} guards $([ "$FAST" -eq 1 ] && echo '(--fast)')"
> echo "──────────────────────────────────────────────────────"
> for i in "${!NAMES[@]}"; do
>   name="${NAMES[$i]}"; cmd="${CMDS[$i]}"
>   echo ""
>   echo "▶ [$((i + 1))/${TOTAL}] ${name}"
>   if out=$(eval "$cmd" 2>&1); then
>     echo "  ✅ GREEN"
>     ROWS+=("✅ ${name}")
>   else
>     code=$?
>     FAILS=$((FAILS + 1))
>     ROWS+=("🔴 ${name} (exit ${code})")
>     echo "  🔴 RED (exit ${code}) — last lines:"
>     echo "$out" | tail -12 | sed 's/^/     /'
>   fi
> done
> ```

> ```
> /** JWT verify + ADMIN gate shared by upload (POST) and media (GET). Token from the Authorization header,
>  *  with a `?token=` query fallback for <video src> (which cannot send headers). Admin-only + owner-scoped
>  *  downstream, same-origin TLS → acceptable for the single-tenant front-door (documented in the SOP). */
> async function authenticate(req: Request, url: URL): Promise<{ userId: string } | Response> {
>   const header = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
>   const token = header.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice(7) : (url.searchParams.get('token') || '');
>   if (!token) return json(401, { error: 'unauthorized' });
>   let userId: string;
>   try {
>     const { data: { user }, error } = await admin.auth.getUser(token);
>     if (error || !user) return json(401, { error: 'unauthorized' });
>     userId = user.id;
>   } catch {
>     return json(401, { error: 'unauthorized' });
>   }
>   // ADMIN-only (matches useIsAdmin — user_roles role='admin').
>   const { data: adminRow } = await admin.from('user_roles').select('role').eq('user_id', userId).eq('role', 'admin').maybeSingle();
>   if (!adminRow) return json(403, { error: 'admin_only' });
>   return { userId };
> }
> 
> /** GET /api/host-media?key=<uid>/<file> — stream a host-inbox master to the browser with Range support
>  *  (HTTP 206 → video seeking). Owner-scoped: the key MUST live under the caller's own inbox dir. */
> function serveMedia(userId: string, url: URL, req: Request): Response {
>   const key = url.searchParams.get('key') || '';
>   if (!key || key.includes('..') || !key.startsWith(`${userId}/`)) return json(400, { error: 'bad_key' });
>   const path = join(INBOX_BASE, key);
>   if (!resolve(path).startsWith(INBOX_BASE + '/')) return json(400, { error: 'bad_path' });
>   if (!existsSync(path)) return json(404, { error: 'not_found' });
>   const size = statSync(path).size;
>   const type = path.endsWith('.mp4') ? 'video/mp4' : 'application/octet-stream';
>   const base = { 'Accept-Ranges': 'bytes', 'Content-Type': type, 'Cache-Control': 'private, no-store' };
> 
>   const range = req.headers.get('range');
>   const m = range?.match(/^bytes=(\d*)-(\d*)$/);
>   if (m && (m[1] || m[2])) {
>     const start = m[1] ? Math.min(parseInt(m[1], 10), size - 1) : Math.max(0, size - parseInt(m[2], 10));
>     const end = m[1] && m[2] ? Math.min(parseInt(m[2], 10), size - 1) : size - 1;
>     if (start > end) return new Response(null, { status: 416, headers: { 'Content-Range': `bytes */${size}` } });
>     return new Response(Bun.file(path).slice(start, end + 1), {
>       status: 206,
>       headers: { ...base, 'Content-Range': `bytes ${start}-${end}/${size}`, 'Content-Length': String(end - start + 1) },
>     });
>   }
>   return new Response(Bun.file(path), { status: 200, headers: { ...base, 'Content-Length': String(size) } });
> }
> === hook ===
>   });
> }
> 
> // Drag-and-drop upload of a LARGE master straight to the host disk (repurpose-inbox/<uid>/) via the
> // nginx-fronted host-upload service — the free-plan Supabase 50MB cap makes Storage impossible for a full
> // documentary. XHR (not fetch) so we get upload progress. The Supabase JWT authenticates + admin-gates server-side.
> // Cloudflare (free plan) caps each request body at 100MB → slice the master into <100MB chunks; each passes CF
> // and the host appends them (renaming on the last chunk). Small files go as a single request. XHR gives progress.
> const CHUNK_BYTES = 80 * 1024 * 1024;
> 
> interface UploadResp { ok?: boolean; done?: boolean; filename?: string; bytes?: number }
> 
> function xhrPost(body: Blob, headers: Record<string, string>, onLoaded: (loaded: number) => void): Promise<UploadResp> {
>   });
> }
> 
> // FR-SPACES-031 reuse (S1 UX): send one produced clip to the existing distribution sink.
> // publish-space-asset resolves the asset SERVER-SIDE owner-scoped and enqueues scheduled_posts (auto-publish drives it).
> export function usePublishSpaceAsset() {
>   return useMutation<
>     { ok?: boolean; variant_id?: string; scheduled_post_id?: string },
>     Error,
>     { source_asset_id: string; platform: string; caption?: string }
>   >({
>     mutationFn: async ({ source_asset_id, platform, caption }) => {
>       const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('publish-space-asset', {
>         body: {
>           source_asset_id, platform, channel: platform, surface: 'video', publish: true,
>           native_text: caption ? { caption } : {},
>         },
>       });
>       if (error) throw new Error(await edgeErrorMessage(error, 'Falha ao enviar para distribuição.'));
>       return data ?? {};
>     },
>   });
> }
> 
> // S1 UX: honest SRT badge — server truth from the ingested master's metadata (RLS own).
> export function useMasterSrtStatus(assetId: string | null) {
>   return useQuery({
>     queryKey: ['master_srt', assetId],
>     enabled: !!assetId,
>     queryFn: async () => {
>       const { data, error } = await supabase
>         .from('creative_assets').select('metadata').eq('id', assetId!).maybeSingle();
>       if (error) throw error;
>       const srt = (data?.metadata as { srt?: { pt?: string; en?: string } } | null)?.srt;
>       const len = (srt?.pt ?? srt?.en ?? '').length;
>       return { hasSrt: len > 100, chars: len };
>     },
>   });

A proveni\u00eancia N\u00c3O \u00e9 este campo | migration `20260712120000` + `05-sdd.md \u00a75.1` |\n| `parent_asset_id` | Lineage: derivado \u2192 master (FK self-referencing `ON DELETE SET NULL`, da origem) | `bridge:106,123` |\n| `source_job_id` | Job de origem: `render_id` da fila \u2014 \u00e9 a **chave de resolu\u00e7\u00e3o do carrossel** (slides de um render = 1 post) | `bridge:106,123` \u00b7 `publish-space-carousel:57-60` \u00b7 `auto-publish:137` |\n| `metadata` | Master: `{provider:'upload', ingested_at, episode, srt}` (schemas em \u00a74). Derivados: `{repurpose:true|carousel:true, source_asset_id, clip_index|slide_index, render_id}` | `ingest:143` \u00b7 `bridge:109,126` |\n| `duration_seconds`/`width`/`height`/`file_size_bytes` | Preenchidos pelo worker a partir do resultado FFmpeg real (nunca declarados pelo cliente no derivado) | `bridge:107,124` |\n\n**RLS (herdada, inalterada):** SELECT = own-or-org (`is_org_member`); **zero policy de INSERT/UPDATE para `authenticated`** \u21d2 default-deny \u2014 o \u00fanico escritor \u00e9 a RPC `register_creative_asset` (SECURITY DEFINER, service-role-only). DELETE own permitido (registro; objeto Storage \u00e9 preocupa\u00e7\u00e3o \u00e0 parte). `UNIQUE (storage_bucket, storage_key)` = \u00e2ncora de idempot\u00eancia do dual-write. Hardening de signed-URL owner-scoped: migrations `20260702230000` + `20260703030000` (fora deste m\u00f3dulo, mas governam a leitura destes assets \u2014 SOP `private-bucket-asset-signing.md`).\n\n### 2.2 \ud83d\udfe1 `video_renders` (REUSA-ESTENDE \u2014 FR-VR-003/006)\n\nTabela existente (origem `20260622040000`; `composition` jsonb + bucket privado de `20260624120000`). **Nenhuma coluna nova.** Uso pelo m\u00f3dulo:\n\n| Column | Uso pelo video-repurpose | Evid\u00eancia |\n|--------|--------------------------|-----------|\n| `engine` | `'repurpose'` \u2014 o worker `video-repurpose-bridge` s\u00f3 faz claim dessas linhas | migration `20260712130000` \u00b7 `bridge:159,162` |\n| `state` | `queued \u2192 running \u2192 done|failed` (claim at\u00f4mico `.eq('state','queued')`; reaper 15 min devolve `running` \u00f3rf\u00e3o a `queued`) | `bridge:50-56,157-159` |\n| `charged_mco` | **`0` em todo enqueue** \u2014 rail FFmpeg gr\u00e1tis (NFR); `finalize` com `p_refund: 0` (sem money-mint) | `video-repurpose-run:139-141` \u00b7 `bridge:140-143,149` |\n| `composition` | O cut-spec/slide-spec sanitizado (schemas em \u00a74) \u2014 o worker consome e RE-VALIDA a fonte no read (OTD-VR-006) | `video-repurpose-run:135-137` \u00b7 `bridge:67-93` |\n| `storage_key` | Setado no `done` = primeiro item do manifest | `bridge:141` |\n| `qa` | Manifest completo `{clips:[{index,storage_key,width,height,bytes,kind}]}` | `bridge:142` |\n| `node_id` | N\u00f3 de observa\u00e7\u00e3o da malha inserido pelo worker | `bridge:132-137` |\n\n**RLS (herdada, inalterada):** SELECT own-or-org; default-deny writes (edge/worker escrevem via service key); **RESTRICTIVE no-delete** (fam\u00edlia ledger financeiro). O cliente faz poll da pr\u00f3pria linha (transporte `poll://<render_id>`, `video-repurpose-run:147`).\n\n### 2.3 \ud83d\udfe2 `scheduled_posts` (REUSA sem migration \u2014 FR-VR-007 agendado / FR-SPACES-079/080)\n\nContrato consumido (nunca criado aqui): INSERT service-role com o **GROUP MARKER** \u2014\n\n```jsonc\n// scheduled_posts (linha enfileirada por publish-space-carousel:77-87)\n{\n  \"user_id\": \"<caller>\",           // server-trusted do JWT verificado\n  \"platform\": \"instagram\",\n  \"scheduled_at\": \"<publish_at ISO | now()>\",\n  \"status\": \"queued\",\n  \"metadata\": {\n    \"reshape\": {\n      \"content\": { \"text\": \"<caption \u22642200>\" },\n      \"carousel_render_id\": \"<render_id>\",   // MARKER, nunca ref de asset (FMEA-011)\n      \"channel\": \"instagram\",\n      \"surface\": \"carousel\"\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n`auto-publish` resolve o marker **owner-bound** (`.eq('source_job_id', marker).eq('user_id', post.user_id)` \u2014 `auto-publish/index.ts:133-141`), assina **fresh 6h a cada tentativa** (`:143-147`; URL de enqueue-time morreria no retry) e lan\u00e7a erro estruturado se <2 slides resolverem/assinarem (`:148-151`) em vez de cair no branch de imagem \u00fanica. Anti double-enqueue: 1 carrossel `queued` por render por user \u2192 `409 already_queued` (`publish-space-carousel:68-75`).\n\n### 2.4 \ud83d\udfe2 Reusadas sem migration (contrato de leitura/escrita)\n\n| Objeto | Contrato consumido | FR / Evid\u00eancia |\n|--------|--------------------|----------------|\n| `profiles.mco_balance` + `deduct_mco_coins` (RPC) + `refundMco` (`_shared/billing.ts`) | Pr\u00e9-check + d\u00e9bito at\u00f4mico `DETECT_COST=3` + refund-on-fail no catch | FR-VR-010 \u00b7 `detect-viral-moments:126-130,264` |\n| `decrypted_user_api_keys` | Gate BYOK fail-closed: `aiKey = openrouter_api_key \\|\\| groq_api_key \\|\\| google_api_key`; aus\u00eancia \u2192 `402 {error:\"ai_not_configured\"}` (contrato de erro REAL \u2014 n\u00e3o `<provider>_not_configured`) | FR-VR-010 \u00b7 `detect-viral-moments:113-123` (reparo `a729f83`) |\n| `user_roles` | Admin-gate do source `bucket='local'` em TR\u00caS pontos (ingest, enqueue, host-upload/media) | `ingest:103-104` \u00b7 `video-repurpose-run:126-127` \u00b7 `host-upload-server.ts:75-77` |\n| `mcorch_nodes` | 2 fam\u00edlias de observa\u00e7\u00e3o: `detect-viral-moments:<masterId>` (`detect:254-259`) e `video-repurpose:<renderId>` (`bridge:133-137`) | Mesh Connection Mandate |\n| `infra_health_logs` | `service \u2208 {ingest-external-asset, detect-viral-moments, publish-space-carousel, video-repurpose-bridge, host-upload}`; o bridge usa `event`+`metadata` (migration `20260615170000`) | `bridge:46-48` |\n| `finalize_video_render` (RPC, `20260622040000`) | \u00daNICA autoridade terminal+refund (service-role; guard `state IN (queued,running,\u2026)` \u21d2 1\u00ba finalizador vence, idempotente) | `bridge:140-143,149` |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Adiciona exatamente UM valor ('external').\nALTER TABLE public.creative_assets DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS creative_assets_source_module_check;\nALTER TABLE public.creative_assets\n  ADD CONSTRAINT creative_assets_source_module_check\n  CHECK (source_module IN ('canvas-studio','hyperframes','open-design','content-pipeline',\n                           'generate-image','faceless','spaces','avatar-studio','external'));\n\n-- register_creative_asset re-selada com a MESMA union +'external' na valida\u00e7\u00e3o interna.\n-- Corpo byte-id\u00eantico fora da lista: SECURITY DEFINER, search_path='', ON CONFLICT\n-- (storage_bucket,storage_key) DO UPDATE ... WHERE creative_assets.user_id = EXCLUDED.user_id\n-- (guard de tenant no upsert), EXECUTE revogado de PUBLIC/anon/authenticated, GRANT s\u00f3 service_role.\n```\n\n### 3.2 \ud83d\udfe1 `20260712130000_video_renders_repurpose_engine.sql` (FR-VR-003)\n\n```sql\n-- ANTICORPO: partir da defini\u00e7\u00e3o DEPLOYADA ('faceless','hyperframes','qwen3-voice'). +1 ('repurpose').\nALTER TABLE public.video_renders DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS video_renders_engine_check;\nALTER TABLE public.video_renders\n  ADD CONSTRAINT video_renders_engine_check\n  CHECK (engine IN ('faceless','hyperframes','qwen3-voice','repurpose'));\n-- Sem mudan\u00e7a de RLS/RPC/grant. O host worker video-repurpose-bridge.ts faz claim de\n-- engine='repurpose' (rail gr\u00e1tis, charged_mco=0).\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. jsonb Schemas (contratos que hoje vivem em c\u00f3digo \u2014 consolidados aqui)\n\n### 4.1 `video_renders.composition` \u2014 modo `repurpose` (cut-spec)\n\nSanitizado no enqueue (`video-repurpose-run:46-61`), re-validado no read pelo worker (OTD-VR-006):\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"mode\": \"repurpose\",\n  \"source\": { \"bucket\": \"<allowlist|local>\", \"key\": \"<${uid}/\u2026>\" },  // re-validado 2\u00d7 (enqueue + read)\n  \"source_asset_id\": \"<uuid do master>\",\n  \"fps\": 30,                        // clamp 1..60, default 30 (CFR)\n  \"clips\": [                        // 1..MAX_CLIPS=50\n    {\n      \"in_sec\": 12.4, \"out_sec\": 41.0,          // finitos, in<out, in\u22650\n      \"reframe\": \"9:16\",                        // \"9:16\" | \"1:1\" (default 9:16)\n      \"caption\": \"<\u2264300 chars>\",                // opcional (drawtext)\n      \"caption_mode\": \"beats\",                  // \"beats\" | \"drawtext\" | \"none\" (FR-VR-014)\n      \"text_beats\": [                           // s\u00f3 com caption_mode='beats'; \u2264MAX_BEATS=12\n        { \"t_start\": 0.0, \"t_end\": 3.1,         // clip-relativos; contidos na janela (\u00b10.05)\n          \"phrase\": \"<\u2264120 chars \u2014 VERBATIM do SRT (Lei 1)>\",\n          \"emphasis\": \"hook\" }                  // \"hook\" | \"punch\" | \"normal\"\n      ]\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 `video_renders.composition` \u2014 modo `carousel` (slide-spec)\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"mode\": \"carousel\",\n  \"source\": { \"bucket\": \"\u2026\", \"key\": \"\u2026\" },\n  \"source_asset_id\": \"<uuid do master>\",\n  \"handle\": \"<\u226440 chars>\",          // handle da marca desenhado no rodap\u00e9 (opcional)\n  \"slides\": [                       // 1..MAX_SLIDES=10 (IG max)\n    { \"t_sec\": 95.0, \"caption\": \"<\u2264300 chars>\" }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 `creative_assets.metadata` do MASTER (ingest)\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"provider\": \"upload\",\n  \"ingested_at\": \"<ISO>\",\n  \"episode\": {                      // \u2264MAX_META_BYTES=256KB \u2014 schema espelha episodios/epNN.json (GabrielAI)\n    \"titulo\": \"\u2026\", \"subtitulo\": \"\u2026\",\n    \"atos\": [ \u2026 ], \"creditos\": { \"blocos\": [ \u2026 ] },\n    \"teaser\": \"\u2026\", \"tags\": [ \u2026 ]\n  },\n  \"srt\": { \"pt\": \"<SRT \u2264512KB>\", \"en\": \"<SRT \u2264512KB>\" }   // inline (canvas-assets rejeitaria MIME .srt)\n}\n```\n\nO SRT inline \u00e9 o **transcript-gate** do detector: `detect-viral-moments` l\u00ea `metadata.srt.pt ?? .en` e retorna `422 no_transcript` sem ele (`detect:91-101`). Master sem SRT \u2192 motor ASR self-host (whisper.cpp) semeia `metadata.srt.pt` \u2014 SOP `asr-master-to-srt.md`.\n\n### 4.4 `creative_assets.metadata` dos DERIVADOS (worker)\n\n```jsonc\n// short:  { \"repurpose\": true, \"source_asset_id\": \"<master>\", \"clip_index\": 0, \"render_id\": \"<render>\" }\n// slide:  { \"carousel\": true,  \"source_asset_id\": \"<master>\", \"slide_index\": 0, \"render_id\": \"<render>\" }\n```\n\n### 4.5 Layout de `storage_key` (namespacing owner-scoped)\n\n| Objeto | Key | Quem for\u00e7a |\n|---|---|---|\n| Master (signed upload) | `${uid}/repurpose-masters/<ts>-<nome-sanitizado \u2264120>` | server-forced (`ingest:61-64`) |\n| Master (host inbox) | \u2b1b `repurpose-inbox/<uid>/<file>.mp4` (disco, n\u00e3o Storage) | host-upload-server + realpath containment |\n| Short derivado | `video-studio-assets` \u2192 `${uid}/repurpose/${renderId}/clip_NNN.mp4` | worker (`bridge:117`) |\n| Slide derivado | `video-studio-assets` \u2192 `${uid}/repurpose/${renderId}/slide_NNN.jpg` | worker (`bridge:100`) |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Buckets & host storage\n\n| Superf\u00edcie | Tipo | Papel no m\u00f3dulo | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `video-studio-assets` | Storage **privado** (criado em `20260624120000` \u2014 reuso) | Output de TODOS os derivados + destino do `sign_upload` do master | default-deny herdado (bucket-scoped RLS); leitura s\u00f3 por signed URL owner-scoped |\n| `canvas-assets` | Storage **privado** (reuso) | Master pequeno subido pelo client sob `${uid}/external/` | allowlist no ingest + prefixo `${uid}/` |\n| `'local'` | \u2b1b **pseudo-bucket** (n\u00e3o existe em `storage.buckets`) | Marca que o master vive no disco do host (`repurpose-inbox/<uid>/`) | **ADMIN-only em 3 pontos** (`user_roles role='admin'`): ingest (`:103-104`), enqueue (`:126-127`), read/stream (`host-upload-server:75-77`); realpath-containment no worker (`bridge:76-84`) e no streamer (`host-upload-server:86-88`) |\n\n> **Por que o pseudo-bucket existe:** free-plan Supabase capa upload em 50MB; um document\u00e1rio master tem >1GB (witness EP01 = 1.336.271.927 bytes). O upload chega chunked (~80MB/chunk, sob o cap CF de 100MB/request) via `host-upload-server.ts` (loopback `127.0.0.1:3220` atr\u00e1s do nginx) e o worker l\u00ea do disco. S\u00f3 os derivados (<50MB) v\u00e3o ao Storage. SOP: `repurpose-host-infra-provisioning.md`; infra versionada em `infra/systemd/{video-repurpose-bridge,host-upload}.service` + `infra/nginx/host-upload.location.conf` (caveat Lei 1: o bloco nginx \u00e9 forma **derivada**, n\u00e3o capturada do host).\n\n---\n\n## 6. O que este data model N\u00c3O usa (corre\u00e7\u00f5es de drift \u2014 Lei 1)\n\n| Claim do blueprint | Realidade shipada | Evid\u00eancia |\n|---|---|---|\n| \"Derivados registrados `source_module='external'`\" | Derivados = **`'hyperframes'`**; s\u00f3 o master \u00e9 `'external'` | `bridge:106,123` vs `ingest:136` |\n| \"Carrossel reusa a superf\u00edcie `carousel` do `channel_profiles`\" (OTD-VR-003) | **N\u00e3o passa por `channel_profiles`**: caminho real = `publish-space-carousel` \u2192 `publish-social` (imediato) OU marker `scheduled_posts.metadata.reshape.carousel_render_id` \u2192 `auto-publish` (agendado). A migration `20260628120000_channel_profiles_carousel.sql` segue s\u00f3 LinkedIn/PDF (`grep instagram` = 0 matches). **OTD-VR-003 ABERTA** | `publish-space-carousel:77-87` \u00b7 `auto-publish:133-151` |\n| \"Slides compostos via render-core.ts (HTML\u2192PNG)\" | **FFmpeg puro** (drawbox/drawtext, sem Playwright); tipografia render-core **diferida** (OTD-VR-007, mitiga\u00e7\u00e3o `MAX_CHARS=16`) | `carousel-core.ts:12,23-26` |\n| \"Queima de legenda: `subtitles=<srt>`\" | **`drawtext textfile=` por clipe** com byte-pad UTF-8; o filtro `subtitles=` nunca foi usado | `segment-core.ts:107-118` |\n| Tabela dedicada de repurpose | **Nenhuma** \u2014 spine `creative_assets` + fila `video_renders` reusados | migrations \u00a73 |\n\n---\n\n## 7. GraphRAG Knowledge Nodes\n\n### Node Types (\ud83d\udfe2 reuso \u2014 nenhum node_type novo)\n\n| node_type | Title Pattern | Content | Quem insere |\n|-----------|--------------|---------|-------------|\n| `observation` | `detect-viral-moments:<masterId>` | n\u00ba de janelas + top score + FR-VR-010; `metadata.kind='viral_moments'` | `detect-viral-moments:254-259` (best-effort, nunca bloqueia a resposta) |\n| `observation` | `video-repurpose:<renderId>` | modo + n\u00ba de assets + master; `metadata.kind='video_repurpose'` + manifest | `video-repurpose-bridge:133-137` (o `node_id` volta para `video_renders.node_id` no finalize) |\n\nAmbos s\u00e3o per-user (`user_id` do tenant), embeddings via trigger padr\u00e3o do spine (`embed-mcorch-node`).\n\n---\n\n## 8. Tenant Isolation Map\n\n| Objeto | Selo | `user_id` scope | Escrita do cliente | Guard extra do m\u00f3dulo |\n|--------|------|-----------------|--------------------|------------------------|\n| `creative_assets` | \ud83d\udfe1 | \u2705 (SELECT own-or-org) | **NUNCA** (s\u00f3 RPC service-role) | key server-forced `${uid}/` no sign_upload; upsert com guard de tenant no `WHERE` |\n| `video_renders` | \ud83d\udfe1 | \u2705 (SELECT own-or-org; RESTRICTIVE no-delete) | **NUNCA** (edge/worker service-role) | source resolvido owner-scoped no enqueue (`.eq id .eq user_id`) + re-valida\u00e7\u00e3o no read (OTD-VR-006) |\n| `scheduled_posts` | \ud83d\udfe2 | \u2705 (RLS existente) | Via edge (marker) | resolu\u00e7\u00e3o do marker com hard tenant bind (`.eq user_id`) no auto-publish |\n| `profiles` (mco) | \ud83d\udfe2 | \u2705 | RPC at\u00f4mica apenas | pr\u00e9-check + refund-on-fail |\n| `decrypted_user_api_keys` | \ud83d\udfe2 | \u2705 (view service-side) | \u2014 | lido s\u00f3 server-side (chave nunca chega ao client) |\n| \u2b1b `repurpose-inbox/` | \u2b1b | prefixo `<uid>/` no filesystem | via host-upload (admin-gated) | realpath deve ficar estritamente sob `INBOX_BASE` (anti symlink/traversal) |\n\n**Regra de tenancy:** `user_id` **jamais** vem do body \u2014 no ingest/enqueue/detector/carousel ele \u00e9 a claim do JWT verificado (`getUser`); no worker \u00e9 a coluna da linha claimada; no `auto-publish` \u00e9 `post.user_id` da linha drenada. O `404 source_not_found` do enqueue cobre tamb\u00e9m asset de outro tenant (indistingu\u00edvel \u2014 anti-enumera\u00e7\u00e3o).\n\n---\n\n## 9. Data Quality Rules (bounds impostos por constru\u00e7\u00e3o)\n\n| Objeto | Regra | Enforcement | Erro (contrato REAL) |\n|--------|-------|-------------|----------------------|\n| `composition.clips` | 1..50; `in<out`, `in\u22650`, finitos | `sanitizeClips` no enqueue | `422 cut_spec_empty` / `too_many_clips` / `clip_range_invalid` |\n| `composition.slides` | 1..10 (IG max); `t_sec\u22650` | `sanitizeSlides` | `422 slide_spec_empty` / `too_many_slides` / `slide_time_invalid` |\n| `text_beats` | \u226412/clipe; phrase \u2264120; janela contida no clipe (\u00b10.05); malformado \u00e9 DROPADO, nunca fabricado | `sanitizeBeats` | (silencioso \u2014 drop) |\n| `metadata.episode` | \u2264256KB (byte-size real via Blob) | `sizedObject` no ingest | (campo omitido) |\n| `metadata.srt.{pt,en}` | \u2264512KB por faixa | `sizedString` | (campo omitido) |\n| `storage_key` (ingest) | prefixo `${uid}/` + sem `..` | valida\u00e7\u00e3o expl\u00edcita | `400 invalid_storage_key` |\n| bucket (ingest/enqueue) | allowlist `{canvas-assets, video-studio-assets}` \u222a `'local'`(admin) | Set + admin-gate | `400 invalid_bucket` / `403 admin_only` / `400 source_ref_invalid` |\n| caption do post IG | \u22642200 chars (limite Meta) | slice no seam | \u2014 |\n| carrossel | \u22652 slides para publicar; \u226410 | valida\u00e7\u00e3o no seam + auto-publish | `422 carousel_needs_2_slides` / throw `carousel_slides_unavailable` |\n| double-enqueue | 1 carrossel `queued` por render/user | SELECT guard antes do INSERT | `409 already_queued` |\n| beats (Lei 1) | `phrase` VERBATIM da cue real do SRT; LLM s\u00f3 escolhe **\u00edndices**; \u00edndice inexistente \u00e9 descartado | constru\u00e7\u00e3o server-side (`detect:200-235`) | (anti-fabrica\u00e7\u00e3o por constru\u00e7\u00e3o) |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Retention & Archival\n\n| Dado | Reten\u00e7\u00e3o | Dele\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|------|----------|---------|\n| `creative_assets` (master + derivados) | Indefinida (biblioteca) | DELETE own (registro) + Storage API; CASCADE do user |\n| `video_renders` | Ledger hist\u00f3rico (RESTRICTIVE no-delete) | service-role erasure only |\n| `/tmp/repurpose-<renderId>` (workdir) | Ef\u00eamero | `rmSync` no `finally` do worker (`bridge:151-153`) |\n| \u2b1b `repurpose-inbox/<uid>/` | Manual (disco do host; gitignored) | a\u00e7\u00e3o Sovereign (sem sweep autom\u00e1tico \u2014 gap registrado em `08-quality-metrics.md`) |\n| `infra_health_logs` | 90 dias (pol\u00edtica global) | cron purge |\n| `mcorch_nodes` (observa\u00e7\u00f5es) | Indefinida | com a linha pai |\n\n---\n\n## 11.
