criou o seam `publish-space-carousel` e a **branch CAROUSEL** dentro do `publish-social` existente. O rail IG do `publish-social` tem hoje **3 superf\u00edcies**: REELS (legado), **CAROUSEL** (este m\u00f3dulo, `:204-230`) e STORIES (Amendment 24, `:232-245`).\n\n---\n\n## 2. BPMN-lite Process Maps\n\n### PROC-VR-00: Provisionamento da infra host (pr\u00e9-requisito material)\n\nFluxo completo na SOP `repurpose-host-infra-provisioning.md` (Sequence 1-3 + gates G1-G7). Resumo dos artefatos e gates:\n\n```\n[cp infra/systemd/*.service \u2192 ~/.config/systemd/user/ + daemon-reload + enable --now]\n  \u2502 GATE G1: systemctl --user is-active host-upload video-repurpose-bridge \u2192 active \u00d72\n  \u25bc\n[Reconciliar nginx: location = /api/host-upload \u2194 infra/nginx/host-upload.location.conf]\n  \u2502 GATE G2/G4: curl loopback 3220 \u2192 401 sem JWT \u00b7 curl https://login.mcorch.com/api/host-upload \u2192 401 (n\u00e3o 404/502)\n  \u2502 GATE G3: JWT n\u00e3o-admin \u2192 403 admin_only\n  \u25bc\n[mkdir -p repurpose-inbox]  \u2192 GATE G6 (host-media): Range request \u2192 206 + Content-Range\n```\n\n> **Caveat Lei 1 (da SOP):** as 2 units foram capturadas verbatim do host; o bloco nginx \u00e9 forma **DERIVADA** de `host-upload-server.ts` (`/etc/nginx` n\u00e3o-leg\u00edvel pelo user `ubuntu`) \u2014 o provisionamento reconcilia contra o bloco vivo.\n> **Recovery:** unit em crash-loop \u2192 `journalctl --user -u <unit> -n 50` (falta `.env`? bun no PATH?); G4=502 \u2192 server loopback caiu; G4=404 \u2192 location ausente; G4=403 do Cloudflare \u2192 playbook `wordpress-cf-publish-unblock.md` (Security Level / WAF Skip).\n\n---\n\n### PROC-VR-01: Ingest do master externo (FR-VR-001/002 \u2014 as 3 portas)\n\n```\nSTART (User em /dashboard/repurpose)\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u25b6 PORTA A \u2014 master pequeno (<50MB): client sobe a canvas-assets sob ${uid}/external/\n  \u2502     \u2514\u2500\u25b6 [Edge: ingest-external-asset]  (user-JWT \u2192 getUser)\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u25b6 PORTA B \u2014 master GRANDE (signed upload): body.action='sign_upload'\n  \u2502     \u251c\u2500\u2500 key SERVER-FORCED `${uid}/repurpose-masters/<ts>-<nome\u2264120>`   (ingest:61-64)\n  \u2502     \u251c\u2500\u2500 createSignedUploadUrl no bucket privado video-studio-assets    (ingest:69-72)\n  \u2502     \u2514\u2500\u2500 client uploadToSignedUrl \u2192 chama a fn de novo p/ registrar\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u25b6 PORTA C \u2014 master no DISCO DO HOST (admin-only; document\u00e1rio >1GB):\n  \u2502     \u251c\u2500\u2500 UI fatia o arquivo em chunks de 80MB (CHUNK_BYTES, useVideoRepurpose.ts:68 \u2014\n  \u2502     \u2502    sob o cap Cloudflare de 100MB/request) \u2192 XHR POST /api/host-upload (progresso real)\n  \u2502     \u251c\u2500\u2500 host-upload-server (loopback 3220): JWT verify + ADMIN-gate user_roles (:75-77)\n  \u2502     \u2502    \u2192 appenda chunks \u2192 renomeia no \u00faltimo \u2192 repurpose-inbox/<uid>/<file>.mp4\n  \u2502     \u2514\u2500\u2500 ingest com storage_bucket='local' \u2192 ADMIN-gate de novo no edge (ingest:103-104)\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 (fallback) provider='youtube' \u2500\u2500\u25b6 HTTP 501 youtube_ingest_gated (OTD-VR-001 \u2014 download\n  \u2502        server-side bloqueado por IP datacenter; CTA: upload do MP4)          (ingest:85-91)\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Valida\u00e7\u00f5es: storage_key come\u00e7a com ${uid}/ + sem '..' (400) \u00b7 bucket allowlist (400) \u00b7\n episode \u2264256KB \u00b7 srt_pt/srt_en \u2264512KB (campos oversize s\u00e3o OMITIDOS, nunca 500)]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[register_creative_asset (service-role): source_module='external', kind='video',\n metadata={provider,ingested_at,episode,srt}]                                   (ingest:131-146)\n  \u2502  \u2514\u2500(regErr \u2014 supabase.rpc N\u00c3O lan\u00e7a em erro SQL; checagem expl\u00edcita)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 500 register_failed\n  \u25bc\nEND \u2014 HTTP 200 { ok, asset_id, source_module:'external' } + pulse infra_health_logs\n```\n\n**Gates de verifica\u00e7\u00e3o (materiais):**\n- **G1 owner-scope:** `storage_key` de outro prefixo \u2192 `400 invalid_storage_key` \u2014 smoke `smoke-external-ingest.ts` G2.\n- **G2 registro:** linha `creative_assets` do dono com `source_module='external'` (UUID por SELECT) \u2014 smoke G1.\n- **G3 metadados:** `metadata.episode.atos` + `creditos.blocos` + `srt.pt` presentes \u2014 smoke G3 (o mapeador da Fatia 4, FR-VR-008 **n\u00e3o-shipada**, depender\u00e1 deles).\n- **G4 YouTube gated:** `501 youtube_ingest_gated` \u2014 smoke G4.\n- **G5 RLS:** tenant B n\u00e3o v\u00ea o asset de A \u2014 smoke G5.\n\n**Recovery:** 400 owner-scope \u2192 re-upload sob `${uid}/`; `22023 invalid source_module 'external'` \u2192 migration `20260712120000` n\u00e3o aplicada (apply-gate Sovereign); upload host travando em chunk \u2192 conferir `proxy_request_buffering off` + `client_max_body_size 100m` no bloco nginx vivo.\n\n---\n\n### PROC-VR-02: ASR master\u2192SRT (whisper.cpp US$ 0 \u2014 **manual hoje**, Lei 2 cumprida por SOP)\n\nO detector \u00e9 **transcript-gated** (`422 no_transcript`). Master sem SRT segue a SOP `asr-master-to-srt.md`:\n\n```\n[1. ffmpeg \u2192 WAV 16kHz mono]  GATE: ffprobe duration \u2248 master (\u00b10,5s)\n  \u25bc\n[2. whisper-cli -m ggml-large-v3-turbo-q5_0 -l pt -osrt]   (engine /home/ubuntu/.mcorch/asr-engine/)\n  \u2502  GATE: .srt >20 cues, timestamps crescentes, \u00faltimo \u2248 dura\u00e7\u00e3o\n  \u25bc\n[3. (Camada 2 \u2014 roteiro-autoritativo) reconcile-srt-roteiro.py <srt> <narracao.json>]\n  \u2502  Whisper d\u00e1 o TIMING real; o roteiro d\u00e1 o TEXTO. Cues sem roteiro (cartelas de edi\u00e7\u00e3o) preservadas.\n  \u2502  WITNESS EP01 (2026-07-13): 707/752 palavras alinhadas (94%); ~30 cues reescritas.\n  \u25bc\n[4. Semear creative_assets.metadata.srt.pt do master]\n     GATE: SELECT length(metadata->'srt'->>'pt') > 1000 \u00b7 detector roda sem 422\n```\n\n**Recovery:** transcri\u00e7\u00e3o vazia \u2192 conferir WAV; idioma errado \u2192 `-l pt` for\u00e7ado; OOM \u2192 cair p/ `ggml-medium-q5_0`. **Reviewer gate (G2 da SOP):** Sovereign l\u00ea 3 cues aleat\u00f3rias vs o v\u00eddeo \u2014 transcri\u00e7\u00e3o errada N\u00c3O alimenta o detector (\"lixo entra, lixo viraliza\"). Alternativa pela UI: upload direto de arquivo `.srt/.vtt` (`VideoRepurposePage.tsx:366-376` \u2192 `srt_pt` no ingest) \u2014 foi isso que fechou majoritariamente a OTD-VR-012.\n\n---\n\n### PROC-VR-03: \"Gerar cortes virais\" \u2014 1-clique (FR-VR-015, o caminho feliz completo)\n\n```\nSTART (bot\u00e3o \"Gerar cortes virais\" \u2014 VideoRepurposePage.tsx:142-179, doViralCuts)\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 [se sem asset registrado] ingest do master host-local (PROC-VR-01 porta C) \u2192 asset_id\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Edge: detect-viral-moments]  (user-JWT \u00b7 FR-VR-010)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 resolve master OWNER-SCOPED (.eq id .eq user_id) \u2500\u2500(n\u00e3o \u00e9 seu/n\u00e3o existe)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 404\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 l\u00ea metadata.srt (pt ?? en) \u2500\u2500(ausente/n\u00e3o-parse\u00e1vel)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 422 no_transcript  (transcript-gate)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Cyber-Sentinel inspeciona slice do transcript PR\u00c9-D\u00c9BITO (nunca cobrar request bloqueada) (:104-110)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 gate BYOK fail-closed: aiKey = openrouter || groq || gemini(google_api_key)\n  \u2502      \u2514\u2500(nenhuma)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 402 { error:\"ai_not_configured\", action:\"Configure \u2026 /dashboard/settings\" }\n  \u2502        (contrato REAL; reparo a729f83 2026-07-16: tenant Gemini-only DESTRAVA \u2014\n  \u2502         endpoint OpenAI-compat gemini-2.5-flash, headers OpenRouter condicionados)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 d\u00e9bito at\u00f4mico DETECT_COST=3 (pr\u00e9-check + deduct_mco_coins) \u2500\u2500(insuficiente)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 402\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 LLM (json_object mode + parse defensivo): escolhe TOP-N janelas + \u00cdNDICES de cues (beats)\n  \u2502      Lei 1: text_beats constru\u00eddos VERBATIM das cues reais (:200-235) \u2014 \u00edndice inexistente \u00e9 descartado\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 expans\u00e3o de janela p/ CONTER os beats + clamp ao transcript + piso MIN_CLIP_S=12 /\n  \u2502      teto MAX_CLIP_S=90 (OTD-VR-013, :210-224) \u00b7 score normalizado 0..1 (:238)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500(qualquer falha p\u00f3s-d\u00e9bito)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 refundMco(3) + infra_health_logs unhealthy \u2192 500\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 200 { clips: ClipSpec[], cue_count, new_balance } + observa\u00e7\u00e3o na malha\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[UI monta cut-spec: reframe 9:16 + caption_mode='beats' + text_beats do detector]  (page:162-166)\n  \u25bc\n[Edge: video-repurpose-run]  \u2192 202 { render_id, transport:'poll://<id>' }   (PROC-VR-04 assume)\n  \u25bc\nEND \u2014 progresso da UI ancorado em SINAIS REAIS (resposta do detector \u2192 estado video_renders \u2192\n      contagem de filhos creative_assets), nunca % sint\u00e9tico                    (page:118-140)\n```\n\n**Gates:** 402-antes-de-job (saldo e BYOK verificados antes de qualquer efeito); refund provado no catch (`detect:263-265`); beats verbatim (anti-fabrica\u00e7\u00e3o por constru\u00e7\u00e3o); smoke `smoke-video-repurpose.ts` G1-G5 no seam de enqueue.\n\n**Recovery:** 422 `no_transcript` \u2192 PROC-VR-02; 402 BYOK \u2192 configurar chave em Settings; 500 p\u00f3s-d\u00e9bito \u2192 refund autom\u00e1tico j\u00e1 executado (verific\u00e1vel em `profiles.mco_balance` + `infra_health_logs service='detect-viral-moments' status='unhealthy'`); re-executar \u00e9 seguro (detector \u00e9 stateless \u2014 n\u00e3o enfileira render).\n\n---\n\n### PROC-VR-04: Worker de segmenta\u00e7\u00e3o (FR-VR-003/004/005/011 \u2014 o cora\u00e7\u00e3o FFmpeg)\n\n```\nSTART (systemd --user video-repurpose-bridge.service \u00b7 poll 4s \u00b7 [--once] p/ drain manual)\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[REAPER: running \u00f3rf\u00e3o >15min \u2192 queued]                                    (bridge:157-159)\n  \u25bc\n[CLAIM at\u00f4mico: UPDATE \u2026 SET state='running' WHERE id=? AND state='queued']  (bridge:50-56)\n  \u2502   (0 linhas = outro worker levou \u2014 NO-OP)\n  \u25bc\n[OTD-VR-006 \u2014 RE-VALIDA\u00c7\u00c3O do source no READ (nunca confiar na linha armazenada)]:\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 bucket='local' \u2192 key `${uid}/` + sem '..' + REALPATH estritamente sob INBOX_BASE\n  \u2502      (anti symlink/traversal)                                          (bridge:76-84)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 bucket remoto \u2192 allowlist {canvas-assets,video-studio-assets} + prefixo + '..'\n  \u2502      \u2500\u2500(viola)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 throw source_ref_rejected \u2192 finalize failed          (bridge:86-92)\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 download \u2192 /tmp/repurpose-<renderId>/source.mp4\n  \u25bc\n[segmentVideo (segment-core.ts) \u2014 SERIAL e determin\u00edstico, clipe a clipe]:\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 reframe: expression-crop SOURCE-AGN\u00d3STICO crop='min(iw,ih*ar)':'min(ih,iw/ar)':centered\n  \u2502      + scale force_original_aspect_ratio=increase (9:16 1080\u00d71920 | 1:1 1080\u00d71080)  (:62-67)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 caption_mode='beats' (FR-VR-011): renderAlphaFrames(viral-caption-overlay-9x16, RGBA,\n  \u2502      omitBackground) \u2192 overlay=0:0:format=auto:eof_action=pass em UM pass    (:79-103)\n  \u2502      \u26a0 ANTICORPO: -ss/-t s\u00e3o op\u00e7\u00f5es do INPUT 0 e PRECEDEM seu -i \u2014 sen\u00e3o -t liga ao\n  \u2502        pr\u00f3ximo input (PNG seq) e o master roda at\u00e9 EOF (~500s de arquivo)     (:88-90)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 caption_mode='drawtext'/default: drawtext textfile= por clipe\n  \u2502      \u26a0 ANTICORPO byte-pad UTF-8: drawtext trunca a cauda por (byteLen\u2212codepointLen)\n  \u2502        bytes de acento \u2192 pad com espa\u00e7os (\"not\u00edcia.\" n\u00e3o vira \"not\u00edc\")       (:110-115)\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 caption_mode='none': sem texto\n  \u25bc\n[Por clipe: upload ${uid}/repurpose/${renderId}/clip_NNN.mp4 (bucket privado) +\n register_creative_asset(kind='video', source_module='hyperframes', parent=master,\n source_job_id=render)]                                                     (bridge:117-128)\n  \u25bc\n[Observa\u00e7\u00e3o na malha (video-repurpose:<renderId> + manifest) \u2192\n finalize_video_render(done, storage_key=1\u00ba item, qa=manifest, refund 0)]   (bridge:132-143)\n  \u2502\n  \u2514\u2500(qualquer throw)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 finalize failed (refund = charged = 0) + infra_health_logs\n       'repurpose_failed' {render_id, error} \u00b7 workdir SEMPRE removido no finally\nEND\n```\n\n**Gates:** claim at\u00f4mico (1 worker leva); OTD-VR-006 provado por constru\u00e7\u00e3o; Vision QA do clipe real (witness 2026-07-12: master sint\u00e9tico ESQ|CENTRO|DIR \u2192 9:16 manteve CENTRO); `charged_mco=0` + `p_refund:0` (sem money-mint); worker stale \u2192 `systemctl --user restart video-repurpose-bridge.service` (mem\u00f3ria `reference_hyperframes_worker_restart`: render `done` pode sair no template errado se o worker rodar c\u00f3digo velho).\n\n**Recovery:** `source_ref_rejected`/`source_download_failed` \u2192 re-ingerir (PROC-VR-01) e re-enfileirar; FFmpeg falha em 1 clipe \u2192 job inteiro `failed` (refund 0), re-enfileirar com cut-spec corrigida; worker morto no meio \u2192 reaper devolve a `queued` em \u226415min e outro claim reprocessa (upload com `upsert:true` e registro `ON CONFLICT` = idempotentes).\n\n---\n\n### PROC-VR-05: Carrossel IG \u2014 render + publish imediato E agendado (FR-VR-006/007)\n\n```\n[Enqueue: video-repurpose-run mode='carousel' slides:[{t_sec,caption?}]\u226410 + handle?]  \u2192 202\n  \u25bc\n[MESMO worker/fila: buildCarousel (carousel-core.ts) \u2014 FFmpeg PURO, sem Playwright]:\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 key-frame: -ss <t_sec> -frames:v 1 \u00b7 center-crop 4:5 \u2192 1080\u00d71350        (:66,95-100)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 legenda WRAPPED em TS (wrapCaption, MAX_CHARS=16 conservador \u2014 OTD-VR-007:\n  \u2502     tipografia pixel-perfect via render-core HTML\u2192PNG DIFERIDA) + byte-pad por LINHA (:49-61,77-83)\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 barra transl\u00facida dimensionada ao n\u00ba de linhas + handle da marca em ciano   (:71-91)\n  \u25bc\n[Worker registra N slides kind='image' (source_module='hyperframes', source_job_id=render)]\n  \u25bc\n[Seam: publish-space-carousel (user-JWT)]\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 resolve slides OWNER-SCOPED por (source_job_id=render_id, user_id, kind='image')  (:57-60)\n  \u2502      \u2500\u2500(<2)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 422 carousel_needs_2_slides \u00b7 (>10)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 trunca em 10 (IG max)\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500\u25b6 MODO IMEDIATO: assina cada slide (6h) \u2192 fetch publish-social\n  \u2502      {platform:'instagram', content:{images[], text}, user_id server-trusted}   (:93-112)\n  \u2502\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500\u25b6 MODO AGENDADO (schedule:true \u2014 FR-SPACES-080):\n         \u251c\u2500\u2500 anti double-enqueue: 1 queued por render/user \u2192 409 already_queued     (:68-75)\n         \u251c\u2500\u2500 publish_at inv\u00e1lido \u2192 422 (um \"now\" silencioso surpreenderia o user)   (:43-49)\n         \u2514\u2500\u2500 INSERT scheduled_posts com GROUP MARKER metadata.reshape.carousel_render_id\n               \u26a0 CORRE\u00c7\u00c3O DE DRIFT: N\u00c3O passa por channel_profiles (OTD-VR-003 ABERTA) (:77-87)\n  \u25bc\n[Cron: auto-publish drena scheduled_posts]\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 resolve o marker OWNER-BOUND (.eq source_job_id .eq user_id \u2014 marker sozinho n\u00e3o\n  \u2502     concede NADA; id forjado de outro tenant = 0 linhas)               (auto-publish:133-141)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 assina FRESH 6h A CADA tentativa (retry re-assina)                   (:143-147)\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 <2 slides resolvidos/assinados \u2192 throw carousel_slides_unavailable (nunca cai no\n        branch de imagem \u00fanica)                                            (:148-151)\n  \u25bc\n[publish-social \u2014 branch IG CAROUSEL]  (publish-social:204-230)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 N children: POST /media {image_url, is_carousel_item:true}           (:210-216)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 parent: POST /media {media_type:'CAROUSEL', children:\"id1,id2,\u2026\", caption}  (:217-222)\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 POST /media_publish {creation_id:parent}                             (:224-229)\n       (rail IG completo hoje: REELS legado \u00b7 CAROUSEL este m\u00f3dulo \u00b7 STORIES Amendment 24 :232-245)\nEND \u2014 alcance real GATED na auditoria de app IG (a\u00e7\u00e3o Sovereign, n\u00e3o c\u00f3digo)\n```\n\n**Gates:** smoke `smoke-scheduled-carousel.ts` **9 gates** (S1-S8 + S5b: 401 \u00b7 400 \u00b7 422 <2 slides \u00b7 tenant-gate \u00b7 enqueue+marker \u00b7 409 anti-dup \u00b7 422 publish_at \u00b7 imediato intocado) \u2014 integrado ao `guard-sweep.sh`; witness E2E prod 2026-07-12 (master \u2192 3 slides 1080\u00d71350 registrados + Vision QA no slide real).\n\n**Recovery:** `carousel_slides_unavailable` no auto-publish \u2192 o post falha estruturado (retry re-resolve/re-assina); 409 \u2192 j\u00e1 enfileirado (buscar a linha existente); slides <2 \u2192 o render carousel falhou ou foi de outro render_id \u2014 re-render (PROC-VR-04).\n\n---\n\n### PROC-VR-06: Distribui\u00e7\u00e3o de short (FR-VR-009 \u2014 reuso puro do sink)\n\n```\n[Bot\u00e3o \"Distribuir\" (VideoRepurposePage:585)] \u2192 usePublishSpaceAsset (hook:177-197)\n  \u25bc\n[publish-space-asset (reuso FR-SPACES-031): resolve o clipe SERVER-SIDE owner-scoped \u2192\n enfileira scheduled_posts]  \u2192 auto-publish \u2192 publish-social\n  \u25bc\n[Disclosure sint\u00e9tica PRESERVADA nos rails (o repurpose herda de gra\u00e7a):\n  \u00b7 TikTok: is_aigc:true (publish-social:435) + SELF_ONLY pr\u00e9-audit (:399-407)\n  \u00b7 YouTube: containsSyntheticMedia:true (:539)\n  \u00b7 IG: REELS/CAROUSEL/STORIES \u2014 alcance gated no app audit Meta]\nEND\n```\n\n---\n\n### PROC-VR-07: Loop Vision de qualidade (FR-VR-013 \u2014 **MANUAL por sess\u00e3o**, honesto)\n\n**N\u00e3o existe como c\u00f3digo.** O que o operador executa hoje (e que a automa\u00e7\u00e3o futura dever\u00e1 reproduzir):\n\n```\n[1. Render conclu\u00eddo (PROC-VR-04/05)]\n  \u25bc\n[2. Extrair frames de amostra do short/slide real (ffmpeg -frames:v)]\n  \u25bc\n[3. Vision QA (scripts/qa/vision-qa.ts \u2014 custo 0, VISION_MCP_PAT) com a rubrica viral:\n     hook-2s leg\u00edvel? \u00b7 legibilidade som-off (beats/caption)? \u00b7 ritmo? \u00b7 reenquadre manteve o sujeito?]\n  \u2502   WITNESS EP01 (2026-07-14): 3 shorts avaliados 9.0 / 9.5 / 8.5; Vision 7-8/10\n  \u25bc\n[4. Score abaixo do padr\u00e3o \u2192 ajustar cut-spec/beats/caption \u2192 re-enfileirar (1 itera\u00e7\u00e3o t\u00edpica)]\n  \u2502   (o cap MAX_VIRAL_ITERS=2 do 10-frd \u00e9 DISCIPLINA DO OPERADOR, n\u00e3o c\u00f3digo)\n  \u25bc\n[5.
